## Risks

| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |
|------|--------|------------|
| All three guest-I/O candidates fail the spike | no way to deliver the corpus into the guest | bounded spike with per-candidate evidence; if all fail, stop and escalate with the spike record — do not improvise |
| The multi-hour image builds fail partway | schedule slip | cheribuild is resumable at target granularity; `timeout $CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT` + background logs; failure → planner blocker per house convention |
| `scripts/common/build_corpus.sh` is shared with B1's successor follow-up | merge conflict or lost lane parameterization | this plan lands first (B1's follow-up is the later writer); changes scoped to the lane case + toolchain mapping + bring-up mode; noted in both plans |
| `scripts/common/derive_registry.py` is shared with B1's successor follow-up | merge conflict or lost skip entries | per-(lane,record) table with explicit ownership: the follow-up deletes only the riscv64/aarch64 asmfunc rows; this plan adds only CHERI-lane rows; the riscv64 derived output must stay byte-identical (tester re-runs step2.py) |
| Lane runs while the bench submodule is not at its pin or not `status --short`-clean | cleanliness asserts fail / build races | **State changed since this plan was drafted (audit-verified 2026-10-15): B1 has committed.** `benchmark` HEAD is now `db84315` ("Translate assembly_return_site …"), tracked worktree clean, one commit ahead of origin — so HEAD **no longer equals** `env.sh`'s `BENCH_COMMIT=c2c7039`. Per the user's 2026-10-15 decision this plan **stays at `c2c7039`** for acceptance (the pin bump and the all-lane re-runs are B1's successor env plan; bumping here would drag riscv64/aarch64/x86_64 re-runs into scope). Step 10's prerequisite is therefore mechanical: check the submodule out at `c2c7039` for the run, and require `git -C benchmark status --short` to be **empty** as a *pre-run* precondition — note `--short` reports untracked paths too, and the submodule currently carries four (`.claude/agents`, `.claude/commands`, `.idea`, `.vscode`), so the ignored/untracked set must be settled before the run, not merely asserted after it |
| B1 Step 4e fires (no valid morello purecap body) | morello's two asmfunc records cannot ever pass in this plan's scope | Step 10 contingency: they stay permanent skips with a skip-reason naming B1's deferral; acceptance defers with B1's successor |
| The guest root lacks the dynamic purecap libs the corpus needs | corpus does not run in the guest | the full world is built for both lanes regardless (the sysroot), and Q3 chose the full disk image for both — so the guest roots are complete userlands, not the cheribsdbox minimal set; the spike still records the image's content, and the injection lever for our chosen class is the **`extra-files` directory** (`disk_image.py:171-180`, `BuildDiskImageBase`) — `extra-files-lists` belongs to the *minimal* image class Q3 rejected and does not apply to `disk-image-<xtarget>` |
| The disk-image build bakes the developer's personal SSH public keys into both lane images | private key material authorized for root login inside artifacts that get stamped, hashed and shared | pre-seed `extra-files/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` with the test key's `.pub` only, before the first image build (`disk_image.py:521-541` + `utils.py:266-267`: `query_yes_no(default_result=True)` self-answers in a detached non-tty build); post-build assertion on the image's `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` |
| Guest egress is unconstrained (QEMU's default slirp netdev, `restrict=off`) | benchmark attack code running in the guest can route IP through the host and reach host services | pin the netdev in `env.sh`: `-net none` for non-network winners, `-netdev user,restrict=on,...` for the SSH winner; assert the realized `-netdev` in the spike record |
| A freed-up `make deploy` hides a stale pinned image | the lane runs the wrong artifact | deploy's image leg is hash-aware, not existence-aware; Step 6's evidence notes the pin is re-taken on every rebuild |
| The spike mutates the pinned image (CheriBSD writes fs state, `/var/log`, atime) | every later `run.sh` dies at the pre-boot hash assert | the spike boots with `-snapshot` (or a copy whose path is kept out of the pins) and its evidence asserts the pinned image's sha256 is unchanged afterwards |
| Narrowed `--qemu/targets` bypasses the pin's target-name rewrite | emulator build produces wrong/absent target binaries | the rewrite (`build_qemu.py:426-427`) is gated on `is_default_value` (line 418), so the option must carry the **final** names `riscv64xcheri-softmmu,morello-softmmu`; Step 5's evidence asserts both binaries exist and run `--version` |
| QEMU cannot resolve `edk2-aarch64-code.fd` for the Morello boot | morello lane cannot boot at all | the pinned QEMU ships it (`pc-bios/edk2-aarch64-code.fd.bz2`, verified in the pin); if QEMU's datadir lookup still fails, `qemu-efi-aarch64` (apt, candidate `2025.11-3ubuntu7.3`, currently **not** installed) is the named fallback — a host prerequisite to raise with the user, not to install silently |
| The planner writes build artifacts under `build/` (staged trees, cheribuild output) | boundary confusion — planner authoring outside `./tmp/` | user-directed hosting role (2026-10-14 decision), same pattern as the 2026-10-13 LLVM staging; **no source, config or doc file outside `./tmp/` is authored by the planner** — the scripts remain coder-written and the plan states this split explicitly so it is not read as a boundary breach |
| A hosted build outlives the planner's turn or the session dies mid-build | lost or orphaned build | each hosted step is a detached background job writing to `./tmp/logs/<plan-id>-host-<step>.log` with a Monitor armed; cheribuild is resumable at target granularity, so a re-run continues rather than restarting |
| SSH candidate exposes a service; key material near a stamp | credential leak / exposure | loopback bind only, key under gitignored `build/` with 0600 asserted, never copied into `env.sh` (version_stamp copies env.sh verbatim into every stamp) |
| **The host toolchain drifts ahead of the pinned sources** (found 4×: missing `libcap-ng-dev`, GCC 15 vs. the 2021 makefs fork — *closed by removing the fork*, glibc 2.43's C23 `strchr` vs. the CheriBSD bootstrap, and the makefs `-t msdos` gap) | a hosted build dies mid-flight, and each discovery costs a multi-hour cycle | <!-- UPDATED 2026-10-15 --> each fix is **verified before it is briefed at the point of effect** (class import, direct compile, the real compile line — never merely at the injection point) and each is **escalated to the user** when it would weaken a deliberate upstream check rather than written through silently. The accepted instance is the **C23 CPATH shim** (`CHERI_CROSS_CPATH`, scoped to the CheriBSD legs; see Host Prerequisites), itself proven by a hosted diagnostic that carried the world build past stage 1.1 to 4.2. **Residual risk is unassessed beyond what has been executed**: the shim covers the one diagnostic glibc 2.43's C23 change produces, but no other 2.43 behaviour has been checked. The container-with-older-glibc route remains available if more 2.43 breakage appears |

## Host Prerequisites (user action — installs need sudo)

The audit verified that the pinned cheribuild cannot build **anything** on this host as it stands. Install once, before the hosted Steps 4–6. Every entry was checked MISSING on this host, and every one has an apt candidate.

<!-- UPDATED 2026-10-15: the six became SEVEN — executing Step 5 found `libcap-ng-dev`, which the
     audit's dependency sweep missed (build_qemu.py:181-182 is reached only after libattr passes,
     so the fatal moved one check later each time). The full set for EVERY target this plan invokes
     was then re-enumerated from the pin and verified one by one; libcap-ng-dev was the only other
     gap. Verified present and NOT in this table: libtoolize, autoreconf, aclocal, sed, bzip2,
     ssh-keygen, bsdtar, bmake, and pkg-config pixman-1 / glib-2.0 / libattr / slirp / libarchive.
     UPDATED AGAIN 2026-10-15: SEVEN became FIVE — removing the makefs fork (below) retired
     `libbsd-dev` and `bmake` with it. This table lists exactly what the plan still invokes;
     the two retired packages remain installed on this host and are simply no longer load-bearing. -->
Also note `bzip2` currently resolves to a **conda** copy (`~/tools/miniconda3/envs/dev/bin/bzip2`) because that env is on `PATH` ahead of `/usr/bin`. `check_required_system_tool("bzip2")` accepts it, so it is not a blocker — recorded only because a PATH-shadowed system tool is the kind of thing that bites later.

| Package | Needed by | Reason (source) |
|---|---|---|
| `libarchive-dev` | every CheriBSD build | `pkg-config libarchive` — `BuildFreeBSDBase.check_system_dependencies` fatals on a non-FreeBSD host (`cross/cheribsd.py:630-636`). Only the `libarchive13t64` runtime is installed |
| `libarchive-tools` | `cheribsd-sysroot-<xtarget>` | provides `bsdtar` (`check_required_system_tool("bsdtar", apt="libarchive-tools")`, `cross/cheribsd.py:2248, 2388`) |
| `libpixman-1-dev` | `qemu` build | QEMU's configure requires a system pixman for system emulation (the pin passes no `--disable-pixman`) |
| `libslirp-dev` | `qemu` build | `check_required_pkg_config("slirp", apt="libslirp-dev")` for QEMU ≥ 7.2 (`build_qemu.py:404-409`) |
| `libcap-ng-dev` | `qemu` build | `check_required_pkg_config("libcap-ng", apt="libcap-ng-dev")` inside the Linux-only 9pfs block (`build_qemu.py:179-182`) — **found 2026-10-15 by executing Step 5**, which fataled with `Required pkg-config file for libcap-ng is missing` after libattr passed |

**The `/usr/include/bsd` symlink is RETIRED 2026-10-15.** <!-- UPDATED 2026-10-15: it existed only for the makefs fork, which is now removed entirely (see below). --> The earlier text recorded a genuine host-layout quirk: Ubuntu's `libbsd-dev` ships `bsd/bsd.h` under `/usr/include/x86_64-linux-gnu/bsd/`, while `makefs_linux.py:46-48` tests a hard-coded `Path("/usr/include/bsd/bsd.h")` and fatals when it is absent — so the user created the symlink `sudo ln -s /usr/include/x86_64-linux-gnu/bsd /usr/include/bsd` on 2026-10-15, and `build_image.sh` mirrored that path in its own preflight. **With the fork gone, nothing this plan invokes checks that path**: `makefs_linux.py`'s lines 45/48/66 are the only references to `bsd/bsd.h` and `bmake` anywhere in cheribuild (verified), so the preflight is removed too. The symlink can be left in place harmlessly; `rm /usr/include/bsd` reverses it.

**A host-toolchain workaround, not a package (found 2026-10-15 by executing Step 6).** <!-- CORRECTED 2026-10-15 after attempt 4 --> The world build's stage 1.1 compiles FreeBSD's bootstrap tools with the **host** `/usr/bin/clang` against the host's libc, and this host's **glibc 2.43** declares `strchr` as a C23 `_Generic` macro returning `const char *` for a const argument (`/usr/include/string.h:261-265`). FreeBSD promotes that const-discard to an error unconditionally (`tools/build/mk/Makefile.boot:41`), so `lib/libc/stdlib/getopt.c:89` and `getopt_long.c:494` fail and the world build dies before it starts. Upstream has not met this — **CheriBSD `main` (2026-10-14) still carries the flag** — and the Linux cross-build shim is unaware of the C23 macros, so the workaround is ours. **User decision 2026-10-15: tolerate it, keeping the pinned sources.**
**The mechanism had to change — and why (attempt 4).** The first attempt passed the waiver through `--cheribsd/build-options` as `CWARNFLAGS.clang=…`. It reached the top-level make and **still never reached the compiler**: FreeBSD's build does not propagate arbitrary command-line variables into the `tools/build` sub-make, which recomputes the variable from scratch, and `Makefile.boot:41`'s `+=` runs **last**, so no outside value can win. **No make-variable-based fix can work here.** The shipped mechanism is a **CPATH header shim** (`scripts/cheri/glibc-c23-shim/`, three headers, `CHERI_CROSS_CPATH`), injected with `--cheribsd/extra-env="CPATH=…"` — CPATH is read by the *compiler*, so it needs no make cooperation — and it is **self-scoping**: CPATH is searched after every `-I`, and the cross-build stage resolves `<string.h>` from its own sysroot, so the shim cannot reach the world build.
**Verified at the point of effect.** A hosted diagnostic ran the real `buildworld` with the shim and carried `>>> stage 1.1` through to `>>> stage 4.2` (log `./tmp/logs/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-diag-cpath.log`), past everything attempts 3 and 4 died at, with no diagnostics from `getopt*.c`. `CCC_OVERRIDE_OPTIONS` was tested as an alternative and **rejected**: it works, but prints 2 lines per clang invocation — tens of thousands across a world build.
**Cost, accepted explicitly when the user chose:** the shim restores the pre-C23 declaration for 11 symbols in 3 headers — a deliberate deviation from the host's C23 semantics, confined to the stage that needs it. No global bare `-Werror` is active, so nothing else is affected.
**The alternative offered and declined:** building inside a container with glibc < 2.43 keeps upstream's checks fully intact and remains available if the residual risk materializes.

**`SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` must be neutralised for the CheriBSD legs (found 2026-10-15 by executing Step 6 attempt 6).** `stand/common/newvers.sh:47-51` validates the variable like this: `if [ -n "$SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH" ]; then if ! t=$(date -ur $SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH …)`. `date -r` is **BSD** semantics (argument = epoch seconds); GNU `date -r` reads its argument as a **filename**, so on this host `date -ur 1791763200` fails with `No such file or directory` and the script exits 1 — `vers.c` fails and the world build dies. The variable reaches the build **by inheritance**: `env.sh:56` pins `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=1791763200` (a reproducibility value for the bench's own builds) and **`env.sh:85` exports it**, while `build_image.sh:60` sources `env.sh` — so cheribuild, `make.py`, bmake and `sh newvers.sh` all see it. It was never intended for CheriBSD. **User decision 2026-10-15: "scope it off for the CHERI legs"** — the fix is an **empty** `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=` on the two CheriBSD `run_cheribuild` invocations (empty makes `[ -n … ]` false, so `newvers.sh` takes its `else date` branch), which leaves the shared pin and its export untouched for every other lane. **Verified at the point of effect**, not inferred: re-running the exact failing command with the pin set reproduces `Invalid SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH`/exit 1, and with it empty exits 0 and writes `vers.c`. Blast radius checked before choosing: no in-repo consumer needs the **export** (`scripts/riscv64/build_image.sh:239-245` parses `env.sh` as a file for its stamp and uses `$SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` in-shell from the sourced file; `scripts/riscv64/build_busybox.sh:29` does its own `export`; nothing in the Makefile or the bench submodule reads it). Recorded, not fixed: the pinned tree's own `tools/build/cross-build/date.sh` is a vestigial stub (`exec /bin/date -r0 "$@"`), and `tools/build/beinstall.sh:173` also assumes BSD `date -r`, but that script is an interactive deployment helper outside buildworld.

**The riscv64 BBL firmware build (added 2026-10-16) needs no new host package.** <!-- ADDED 2026-10-16 --> cheribuild's `bbl-baremetal-riscv64-purecap` is a GNU-make/autotools project (`cross/bbl.py`, `make_kind = GnuMake`) built with the already-installed CHERI SDK clang against the `--without-payload` sources; `gmake` is verified present (`/usr/bin/gmake`), and a `--pretend` dry run of the exact target resolves its whole plan without complaint. The only thing it needs that does not yet exist is the **staged source tree** (`sources/bbl`), which Step 1's script provides — and which is load-bearing: `--skip-update` does **not** stop cheribuild from git-cloning a missing tree (`ensure_cloned()` runs before the skip check, `project.py:1354-1362`), so an unstaged tree would silently build the `cheri_purecap` branch **tip** instead of the pin. If a hosted run nevertheless stops on a missing tool, **escalate to the user — never install silently**.

**The `makefs` route — REMOVED 2026-10-15 (Plan Error; user decision).** <!-- UPDATED 2026-10-15: this paragraph previously instructed building cheribuild's `makefs-linux` target and pinning `MAKEFS_CMD` at its output. That instruction was wrong, and following it broke the disk-image step it was meant to protect. --> The earlier text read: build the `github.com/Engil/makefs` fork (`projects/makefs_linux.py:36-38`) rather than the universe `makefs` package, and point `MAKEFS_CMD` at the binary it installs, because `disk_image.py:969` reads that variable from the environment and "does not search `PATH`", so without it the disk-image leg would fatal `Missing makefs command`. **The premise was false.** `disk_image.py` invokes makefs as `-t msdos` to build the disk's EFI FAT partition, and the fork **has no msdos support at all** — zero `msdos` references in its source tree, `Unknown fs type 'msdos'` from the built binary, and its **latest upstream commit is 2021-10-15, exactly the commit this plan pinned**, so it can never gain it. `MAKEFS_CMD` is only an **optional override** (`disk_image.py:969-970`); `:974-979` otherwise falls back to the **FreeBSD host-tools makefs** cheribuild already builds via `LOCAL_XTOOL_DIRS`/`MK_DISK_IMAGE_TOOLS_BOOTSTRAP` (visible in the build log's own `installworld` line: `usr.sbin/makefs usr.bin/mkimg`), searching **`<objdir>/tmp/{legacy/bin,usr/sbin}/makefs`** — its fatal message says the tool "Should be found in FreeBSD build dir". **`<objdir>` is not the build root**: FreeBSD's objdir carries the `MAKEOBJDIRPREFIX` nesting, so the real path (confirmed empirically 2026-10-15) is `<build_root>/cheribsd-<xtarget>-build/home/wsong83/…/build/cheri/sources/cheribsd/riscv.riscv64c/tmp/legacy/bin/makefs` — a check against `<build_root>/tmp/legacy/bin/` finds nothing and must not be mistaken for absence. Both tools resolve this way with no override. The makefs binary is host-runnable (`ELF 64-bit LSB pie executable, x86-64 … for GNU/Linux`) and was **verified at the point of effect** — run with the build's own argv (`-t msdos -s 2m -B le -N <rootfs>/etc …`) it exits 0 and produces a real FAT12 image (`DOS/MBR boot sector … FAT (12 bit)`), where the fork fails outright. `mkimg` is present at the same `tmp/legacy/bin/` level. **User decision 2026-10-15: remove the fork wholly** — target, source pin, staging leg, and the host prerequisites it alone required.
  - **Consequence for the package table above:** `libbsd-dev` and `bmake` are no longer needed by anything this plan invokes (verified: `makefs_linux.py:45,48,66` are their only references anywhere in cheribuild), so the set is **five**, not seven: `libarchive-dev`, `libarchive-tools`, `libpixman-1-dev`, `libslirp-dev`, `libcap-ng-dev`. All seven are installed on this host, which is harmless — the two extra simply stop being load-bearing.
  - **The historical `versions-stage.txt` `makefs` line stays.** That record is append-only by design; it documents a tree that was staged and is now retired, which is precisely what an append-only provenance log is for.

**Security pre-seed — do this before the first image build.** `disk_image.py:521-541`: when `extra-files/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` does not exist, the build globs `~/.ssh/*.pub` and asks `query_yes_no(default_result=True)` — and `utils.py:266-267` returns that default when stdin is not a tty, which is exactly the case for a detached hosted build. Left alone, both lane images would silently gain the developer's personal public keys plus `PermitRootLogin without-password`. Creating `$CHERI_BROOT/sources/extra-files/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` (0600) holding **only** the test key's `.pub` satisfies the `is_file()` guard, suppresses the personal-key path entirely, and provisions Step 7's SSH candidate. A post-build assertion checks the image's `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` contains exactly that key.

## Rollback

| Change | Revert |
|---|---|
| `env.sh` appended pin block | delete the block — the design keeps existing pins untouched, so nothing else needs unwinding |
| `env.sh` timeout-assignment style (`: "${LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT:=3600}"`) | restore the plain assignments; defaults are unchanged either way, so no lane behavior moves |
| The three `scripts/cheri/*.sh` and the lane scripts | `git revert` the coder commits; no shared lane is affected until the Makefile flip lands |
| Makefile CHERI recipes (the flip) | revert the flip — the scaffolds' `not provisioned` path returns and the two lanes are inert again |
| `build/cheri` shared artifacts (staged trees, two emulators, worlds, disk images — tens of GB) | **outside every `clean` target by design** (`make ARCH=cheri_riscv64 clean` removes only `build/cheri_riscv64`). Reclaim with an explicit `rm -rf build/cheri/{sources,output,build}` (keep `cheribuild-src` + `versions*.txt` to avoid re-fetching) — documented here because the plan previously had no reclamation path |
| A rebuilt disk image after a pin change | re-pin procedure: take the new sha256, edit the `env.sh` image pin, re-run Step 1's verify clause; `run.sh`'s pre-boot assert is the check that the re-pin happened |
| `scripts/common/build_corpus.sh` / `derive_registry.py` CHERI additions | disjoint from B1's successor-follow-up entries by design; revert only after that successor lands, or by reverting the disjoint rows |

## Proposed Changes

Each step includes verification checks tagged with confidence. `(auto)` = mechanical, coder self-verifies.

**Steps 1–3 write the scripts; Steps 4–6 execute them under planner hosting.** The coder's checks in Steps 1–3 are therefore **structural and dry-run only** — the scripts are not run to completion in a round (the round's wall clock cannot hold them), and their real execution is evidenced by Steps 4–6. Step 7's spike then consumes the artifacts those hosted steps produced.

1. **Step 1:** Stage sources + pins (script only). Add `scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` as a **git-repository staging** script (design A, user decision 2026-10-15; the codeload tarball route is dropped because cheribuild rejects a tree without `.git` — see the staging-pattern bullet): per tree, `git init` + `git remote add origin <git-url>` + `git fetch --depth 1 origin <commit>` + `git checkout --detach FETCH_HEAD`, then assert `git rev-parse HEAD` equals the pin; every commit pin asserted `^[0-9a-f]{40}$` and every binary name `^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$` before use (die naming the variable, mirroring the Makefile's empty-value refusals); the staged commit's tree hash (`git rev-parse HEAD^{tree}`, which replaces the tarball sha256 this design previously recorded) promoted into a `versions-stage.txt` TOFU record (append-only; never re-invoke `version_stamp.sh` on a shared file). The script is **idempotent**: a staged tree already at the pin is reused, never re-fetched, and `versions-stage.txt` records at most one line per pin (a second run appends nothing). It **takes the shared lane lock** (`$BUILD_DIR/.lane.lock`, `flock -n`, fail fast naming the holder) exactly as the build scripts do — it writes the shared `sources/` tree and the append-only TOFU record, and an unlocked staging run could interleave with a lane build. Stage the **three** pinned trees into `build/cheri/sources/`: `qemu` and `cheribsd` from the Summary's pin table, **plus `bbl`** — the riscv64 lane's boot firmware, source `github.com/CTSRD-CHERI/riscv-pk` @ `cheri_purecap` (added 2026-10-16; the dir name is cheribuild's own — **`bbl`, not `riscv-pk`** — the same trap class as `makefs` vs `makefs-linux`; the repo has **no submodules**, so the same design-A mechanics suffice). It is consumed by the `bbl-baremetal-riscv64-purecap` target that Step 3 now invokes. (The third tree the earlier revision carried — `github.com/Engil/makefs` for the `makefs-linux` target — is **removed**: the fork cannot build the disk's msdos EFI partition, so its staging leg, its assert and its pins are deleted outright; see Host prerequisites.) The script's git use is deliberate and pinned: `git fetch --depth 1 origin <commit>` takes that exact commit and never a branch tip, and the `rev-parse HEAD` assert is the proof of it. (The former "never git-clone GitHub" rule came from the 2026-10-14 sandboxed session whose egress allowlist was `["127.0.0.1:8080", "api.anthropic.com"]`; that constraint does not apply to this session and could not satisfy cheribuild regardless.) Extend `env.sh` with a new appended block: `CHERI_QEMU_COMMIT`, `CHERI_QEMU_URL`, `CHERIBSD_SRC_COMMIT`, `CHERIBSD_SRC_URL` (the two `*_URL` pins hold **git remotes** — `https://github.com/<org>/<repo>` — not codeload tarball routes; their values change in this revision, the commit pins do not), `QEMU_RISCV64XCHERI=qemu-system-riscv64xcheri`, `QEMU_MORELLO=qemu-system-morello`, `QEMU_TARGETS_CHERI=riscv64xcheri-softmmu,morello-softmmu`, the per-lane disk-image pins (path + sha256, filled after Step 6), **the per-lane boot parameters as pins — no script hard-codes them** (the repo doctrine is "all pins live in env.sh": `QEMU_MACH_CHERI_*`, `QEMU_CPU_CHERI_*`, `QEMU_MEM_CHERI_*` sized in Step 7, `QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_MORELLO=edk2-aarch64-code.fd`, **`QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64=bbl-riscv64cheri-virt-fw_jump.bin`** (added 2026-10-16 — a bare datadir filename, mirroring the Morello pin; QEMU resolves it through `qemu_find_file()`, `hw/riscv/boot.c:107-127`), `QEMU_KERNEL_CHERI_RISCV64=<path read back from the built tree in Step 6>`, and the per-lane netdev pins `QEMU_NET_CHERI_*`), the two riscv-pk firmware-source pins (`BBL_SRC_URL` = the git remote, `BBL_SRC_COMMIT` = the resolved `cheri_purecap` tip), the per-lane purecap sysroot pins (asserted against the built tree in Step 6; the target names are `cheribsd-sysroot-riscv64-purecap` and `cheribsd-sysroot-morello-purecap`, and the **install path is not assumed** — the earlier draft's `output/sdk/sysroot-<xtarget>` guess is replaced by reading it back from cheribuild and pinning the observed path), and `CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT` (generous; wraps Steps 2–3) — existing pins untouched **except** the two timeout assignments, which change from plain assignments to `: "${LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT:=3600}"` / `: "${LANE_DEPLOY_TIMEOUT:=7200}"` form so the 124-no-publish exercise can override them (defaults unchanged; see the Summary's timeout bullet).
   → coder verify (scripted): `python3 ./tmp/verification/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-scripts.py` — its Step 1 sections (script existence, lane lock, the git-staging sequence — `.git`/`git init`, depth-1 fetch, `FETCH_HEAD`, `rev-parse HEAD` — 40-hex pin assertion, TOFU record, the git-URL env pins, the **bbl tree staged under cheribuild's own dir name** (asserted with its pins *and* asserted not to be `riscv-pk`), every other new `env.sh` pin — including the two riscv-pk source pins and `QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64`, whose value is asserted to be a **bare datadir filename** and whose commit is asserted to equal the resolved tip — + the two `:=` timeout assignments, and the **absence** of the four removed makefs pins). Proven red against the pre-change tree: 19/19 fail at the original draft; at this revision **91 checks, red at 81/91**, the ten failures being exactly the assertions this round introduces (three bbl env pins, the resolved commit, the bare filename, the staged `bbl` tree, its dir name, the bbl target, the firmware pin and the `share/qemu` read-back).
   → coder verify (auto): `bash -n scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` exits 0; `git diff env.sh` touches only the appended block plus the two timeout assignment lines; every new pin name appears in the block's `export` line; a scratch invocation with an empty `CHERI_QEMU_COMMIT` dies naming the variable (checked without network — the pin assertion precedes the fetch); `grep -c 'codeload' scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` = 0 (the tarball route is gone by design: cheribuild rejects a tree without `.git`); `grep -c 'flock -n' scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'rev-parse HEAD' scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` ≥ 1
   → tester verify: a second `stage_sources.sh` run against an already-staged tree exits 0 and leaves `versions-stage.txt` byte-identical (idempotence, no duplicate TOFU lines)
2. **Step 2:** Emulator build (script only). Add `scripts/cheri/build_emulator.sh`: takes the shared lane lock (`$BUILD_DIR/.lane.lock`, flock -n, fail fast naming the holder), wraps the build in `timeout $CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT`, reuses the exact `CHERIBUILD_LAYOUT` flags from `build_toolchains.sh` (`--source-root "$CHERI_BROOT/sources"`, `--output-root "$CHERI_BROOT/output"`, `--build-root "$CHERI_BROOT/build"`, `--skip-update`) with the `qemu` target and `--qemu/targets="$QEMU_TARGETS_CHERI"` (the **final** target names — see the Risk row); records the two emulator versions into `versions-emulator.txt` (append-only). Two preflights run **before** cheribuild is invoked: (a) the staged source tree must exist (with `--skip-update` a missing tree still makes cheribuild try to clone), dying and naming it otherwise; (b) the qemu-specific host prerequisites — `pkg-config --exists slirp pixman-1`, dying with the apt package names (`libslirp-dev`, `libpixman-1-dev`) — because the pinned cheribuild's `BuildQEMU` calls `check_required_pkg_config("slirp", apt="libslirp-dev")` unconditionally for QEMU ≥ 7.2 (`build_qemu.py:404-409`) and QEMU's configure needs pixman for system emulation. Long-running (tens of minutes with the narrowed list; hours if widened) — Step 5 hosts it; failure = planner blocker.
   → coder verify (scripted): `python3 ./tmp/verification/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-scripts.py` — its Step 2 section (staged-tree assert, `--skip-update`, the `--qemu/targets` narrowing, the pkg-config preflight naming both apt fallbacks).
   → coder verify (auto): `bash -n` passes; the script names both `$QEMU_TARGETS_CHERI` members; `grep -c 'skip-update' scripts/cheri/build_emulator.sh` ≥ 1; the script asserts `build/cheri/sources/qemu` exists and dies naming it when absent (scratch run with the tree temporarily unseen); `grep -c 'pkg-config' scripts/cheri/build_emulator.sh` ≥ 1 and the preflight dies naming both apt packages when run on a host without them; no `git clone`
3. **Step 3:** Guest image builds (script only). Add `scripts/cheri/build_image.sh` (same lane lock + `timeout $CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT` + the same `CHERIBUILD_LAYOUT` + `--skip-update`). It runs in this order:
   - **Preflights**, mirroring Step 2's shape: the staged `cheribsd` tree must exist (die naming it — `--skip-update` still clones a missing tree); `--list-targets` must name every target this script will invoke, and a mismatch is a **hard failure**, not just a logged record; and the CheriBSD host prerequisites must be present — `pkg-config --exists libarchive`, `command -v bsdtar` — dying with the apt package names (`libarchive-dev`, `libarchive-tools`). The `bmake` and `/usr/include/bsd/bsd.h` preflights are **gone with the fork** (2026-10-15): nothing this script still invokes reads either. Without the libarchive check the first world build dies in `BuildFreeBSDBase.check_system_dependencies` (`cross/cheribsd.py:630-636`) after the lock has been taken.
   - **No makefs leg** (this bullet replaces the former "`makefs-linux` first" instruction, removed 2026-10-15): the disk-image leg uses the **FreeBSD host-tools makefs** cheribuild builds itself, discovered by `disk_image.py:974-979` under `<objdir>/tmp/{legacy/bin,usr/sbin}/makefs` — with `<objdir>` the nested `MAKEOBJDIRPREFIX` path, not the build root (see Host prerequisites for the confirmed full path). `MAKEFS_CMD` is only an **optional override**, and the script must set **no** such override: the `MAKEFS_CMD="$ROOT/$MAKEFS_CMD"` prefix on the `run_cheribuild` helper is exactly what broke attempt 5 and it is deleted (leaving it while removing the pin would kill the script on an unbound variable under `set -u` — invisible to `bash -n`).
   - **Inject the host-toolchain C23 shim on the CheriBSD legs** (added 2026-10-15; mechanism corrected after attempt 4 — see Host Prerequisites, a make-variable fix provably cannot work): `--cheribsd/extra-env="CPATH=$ROOT/$CHERI_CROSS_CPATH"` on both CheriBSD lane invocations, pointing at `scripts/cheri/glibc-c23-shim/`, so stage 1.1's bootstrap tools compile against this host's glibc 2.43. **`SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` is UNSET for the cheribuild invocation** (added 2026-10-15 after attempt 6; **mechanism corrected after attempt 7**): `run_cheribuild` runs cheribuild as `env -u SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH …`, because this plan's `env.sh` exports that pin and FreeBSD's `newvers.sh` validates it with BSD `date -ur`, which GNU `date -r` cannot satisfy (see Host Prerequisites). **An empty value does not work** — clang validates the variable too and rejects `''` (`must be a non-negative decimal integer <= 253402300799`); only *unset* satisfies both consumers. The `extra-env` value therefore carries `CPATH` alone — and it must stay a single value, since a second `--cheribsd/extra-env` flag overwrites the first (verified with `--pretend`: the two-flag spelling silently dropped `CPATH`). It is passed to the two CheriBSD lane invocations and to no other leg — with `makefs-linux` gone (2026-10-15) there is no other leg it could reach. The shim directory also joins the preflights (die naming it when absent), mirroring the staged-tree checks. One invocation per lane is sufficient, so the world-first ordering is untouched.
   - **Pre-seed the SSH extra-files guard** (security, mandatory regardless of the spike's winner): create `$CHERI_BROOT/sources/extra-files/root/.ssh/authorized_keys`, mode 0600, containing **only** the test key's `.pub` (`build/cheri/build/insecure_test_ssh_key.pub`). This satisfies the `is_file()` guard at `disk_image.py:521` so the build cannot self-answer "yes" to globbing the developer's personal `~/.ssh/*.pub` (see the Risks row).
   - **Then the per-lane target sets**: `cheribsd-sysroot-<xtarget>` (the world build that also produces the purecap sysroot) and `disk-image-<xtarget>` (the bootable disk), i.e. `cheribsd-sysroot-riscv64-purecap` + `disk-image-riscv64-purecap`, and `cheribsd-sysroot-morello-purecap` + `disk-image-morello-purecap` — **plus, added 2026-10-16, the riscv64 boot firmware `bbl-baremetal-riscv64-purecap`** (listed **first**: cheap, and it is the riscv64 boot's prerequisite). It lives in **this** script, not `build_emulator.sh`, because the image leg is where this plan gathers and pins the *boot vehicles* (the riscv64 `-kernel`, the disk images); the emulator leg stays "the two binaries". Its source tree (`sources/bbl`) is staged by Step 1, and — verified 2026-10-16 — the pinned cheribuild's `--list-targets` names this target verbatim, so the existing hard-fail preflight loop covers it with no special case. Multi-hour per lane; Step 6 hosts it; outputs stay under `build/cheri/output`.
   - **Records artifacts** into `versions-image.txt` and fills the `env.sh` pins (Step 1's block) with observed values: per-lane disk image path + sha256 (taken from the pristine post-build artifact), the sysroot install path **as built**, the **riscv64 boot kernel path** (`QEMU_KERNEL_CHERI_RISCV64` — the riscv64 lane's `-kernel` input, read back from the built tree; the earlier draft recorded a "Morello boot kernel" instead, which was backwards — Morello boots UEFI+`loader(8)` from the disk and needs none), the Morello firmware filename, and — **added 2026-10-16** — the **riscv64 boot firmware** (`share/qemu/bbl-riscv64cheri-virt-fw_jump.bin`): assert it exists after the build (die naming the target otherwise), record its path + sha256, and check the `QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64` pin against the observed file. It is a boot vehicle like the kernel, so it is pinned the same way: **read back, never assume**. A rebuild re-fills these pins (see the image/pin feedback loop).
   → coder verify (scripted): `python3 ./tmp/verification/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-scripts.py` — its Step 3 section (all four CheriBSD targets named, the staged-tree assert, the hard-fail `--list-targets` comparison, both host-prereq preflights, the **absence** of any `MAKEFS_CMD` override, and the mode-0600 test-key-only extra-files pre-seed).
   → coder verify (auto): `bash -n` passes; the script names all four CheriBSD targets **plus `bbl-baremetal-riscv64-purecap`** and no `makefs-linux`; it records a sha256 per lane's disk image and the **observed** sysroot path rather than a hard-coded guess; it records the riscv64 kernel path (not a Morello kernel) **and the riscv64 firmware path, and asserts the firmware file exists before pinning it**; the extra-files pre-seed is present with mode 0600 and the test-key-only content; each preflight dies naming its package/tree when run against a host or tree missing it; `grep -c 'skip-update' scripts/cheri/build_image.sh` ≥ 1; no `git clone`
   → tester verify: after a build, the image's `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` contains exactly the test key (no personal keys), and the build log shows the disk-image leg selecting its makefs from the FreeBSD build dir with no `MAKEFS_CMD` in the environment
4. **Step 4:** *(planner-hosted)* **Confirm the host prerequisites are installed** (Host Prerequisites section — the five apt packages), then fetch the two source trees. Execute `scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` (user decision Q1: the planner hosts network work) as a foreground Bash call — this is minutes, not hours — and capture its log to `./tmp/logs/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-fetch.log`. If any prerequisite is still missing, **stop and ask the user** rather than installing it.
   → Evidence: the five packages verified present (`dpkg -l` output captured); the script's exit status recorded in the plan; `build/cheri/sources/{qemu,cheribsd}` exist; `versions-stage.txt` carries both commit ids and their observed **tree hashes** (the git-staging TOFU value — "tarball sha256" was the pre-design-A phrasing, corrected here); the two pins in `env.sh` equal the Summary pin table
5. **Step 5:** *(planner-hosted)* Build the two emulators. Execute `scripts/cheri/build_emulator.sh` as a **detached background job** with a Monitor armed on its log (the build outlives a single turn).
   → Evidence: exit status; `build/cheri/output/sdk/bin/qemu-system-riscv64xcheri --version` and `.../qemu-system-morello --version` both exit 0 with the version string captured; `pc-bios/edk2-aarch64-code.fd` present in the built tree's datadir (the Morello firmware the Risk row depends on)
6. **Step 6:** *(planner-hosted)* Build the two lanes' CheriBSD worlds, sysroots and disk images (no `makefs-linux` leg — removed 2026-10-15; the disk-image step discovers cheribuild's own FreeBSD host-tools makefs). Execute `scripts/cheri/build_image.sh` as a detached background job with a Monitor armed; resumable at target granularity if interrupted. **If Step 7's spike later selects a mechanism needing new image content, this step is re-run** (hosted again, minutes on top of the built world) with the staged `extra-files` content — its pin-fill re-takes the sha256 and refreshes `env.sh`; that re-run, not a hand-edit of the image, is the only supported way the pinned artifact changes.
   → Evidence: exit status; the build log shows the disk-image leg discovering the FreeBSD host-tools makefs with no `MAKEFS_CMD` override set; per lane, the disk image exists at **`build/cheri/output/cheribsd-<xtarget>.img`** — cheribuild's own name (`disk_image.py:115-123`), not `disk.img`, which is the mistake attempt 8 exposed — with its sha256 recorded in `versions-image.txt` and the `env.sh` pin naming that same path; the observed purecap sysroot path is recorded and matches the `env.sh` pin; the riscv64 kernel path is recorded and pinned; the image's `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` holds exactly the test key; the `--list-targets` names match the invoked targets; the Morello image contains `/boot/kernel` (its UEFI+`loader(8)` path) and the riscv64 lane has its `-kernel` input; the build log shows the CheriBSD invocations carrying `--cheribsd/extra-env` with the `CPATH` shim, and **stage 1.1's `lib/libc/stdlib/getopt*.c` compiles** (the exact failure of attempts 3 and 4) with the build advancing past stage 4.1; and `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` is **unset** for the cheribuild invocation (`env -u` in `run_cheribuild`; an *empty* value was tried and rejected by clang — see Host prerequisites), with `stand/common/newvers.sh` producing `vers.c` for **both** lanes (the exact failure of attempt 6); and, for the re-run that adds the bbl leg, **`output/sdk/share/qemu/bbl-riscv64cheri-virt-fw_jump.bin` exists** with its sha256 recorded and the `QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64` pin naming it (the exact failure of Step 7's round 1, whose boot could not find that file) <!-- UPDATED 2026-10-16: the SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH wording here still described the superseded empty-value fix; corrected to the proven `env -u` mechanism, and the bbl firmware artifact added. -->
7. **Step 7:** Boot spike + I/O mechanism decision (bounded). Boot each pinned image under its emulator, with `-nographic`, a `timeout`, the Step 1 pins, **and `-snapshot`** — the spike must never mutate the pinned artifact (CheriBSD writes fs last-mounted state, `/var/log` and atime; any in-place change makes every later `run.sh` die at its pre-boot hash assert). Boot parameters (`QEMU_MEM_CHERI_*` etc.) are sized from the measured image rather than copied from the riscv64 lane's 256, and are read from `env.sh` — the spike hard-codes nothing. The per-lane argv is fixed by the Summary's boot table:
   - `cheri_riscv64`: `-M virt -cpu "$QEMU_CPU_CHERI_RISCV64" -bios "$QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64" -kernel "$QEMU_KERNEL_CHERI_RISCV64" -drive file=<pinned disk>,format=raw,if=virtio` (**the firmware, the CPU pin and the kernel entry are all load-bearing** — see the boot-vehicle decision; round 1 proved `-kernel` alone is not enough, and round 26 proved the CPU pin must carry the Sstc workaround or the kernel panics at clock init).
   - `aarch64-morello`: `-M virt,gic-version=3 -cpu morello -bios "$QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_MORELLO" -drive file=<pinned disk>,format=raw,if=virtio`, asserting the firmware resolves and `loader(8)` reaches the kernel.
   - Both: the pinned netdev — `-net none` unless the SSH candidate wins, in which case `-netdev user,restrict=on,...` with the loopback `hostfwd` (see the containment decision; the riscv64 model passes no nets and QEMU then defaults to a `restrict=off` slirp device).
   Test the three candidates in order — (1) 9p with `security_model=mapped-xattr` and the corpus-in share read-only; (2) SSH: loopback-only `hostfwd`, cheribuild's test key at `build/cheri/build/insecure_test_ssh_key{,.pub}` (0600 asserted; the **public key's fingerprint** — never a hash of private material — recorded in `versions-emulator.txt` when selected; the public half is already in the image via Step 3's mandatory pre-seed) — the disk-image build already generates sshd **host** keys (`disk_image.py:1052-1075`); (3) the image's `extra-files` directory for corpus-in, paired with a second out-channel — admissible **only** per the Summary's replaceability rule, i.e. the derived corpus/registry the Step 10 loop rewrites must not be baked. Record per candidate: the mechanism's two-way capability (in AND out), the evidence (command + observed output), the security constraints applied (loopback binding, read-only in-share, netdev containment, regular-file-only collection anchored under the share root, collected artifacts hashed), which parts (if any) must be baked into the image, and the winner(s) — a per-lane winner is allowed. Boot logs of both images saved. The guest driver (`scripts/cheri/guest/runner`) is written here: state per mechanism who triggers it and whether it must be baked.
   → coder verify (auto): a spike record exists naming each candidate's verdict with evidence, the chosen mechanism(s), both boot logs, and the netdev actually realized; the pinned images' sha256 values are unchanged after the spike (asserted, not assumed); a **boot failure** — distinct from "no candidate achieved two-way capability" — stops the step and escalates to the planner with the boot log rather than improvising a boot line; if every candidate fails two-way capability, the step likewise stops and escalates with the spike record
8. **Step 8:** Lane wiring. Add `scripts/cheri_riscv64/{prepare.sh,run.sh}` and `scripts/morello/{prepare.sh,run.sh}`.
   - **prepare.sh** = aarch64-style provisioning (lane lock, toolchain asserts, objdump shim, **host scheduler build** with `ARCH=<token> CXX=g++` only — no enable flag) **plus riscv64-style corpus staging**: derived registry via `derive_registry.py --lane <bench token>`, unit builds through the extended `build_corpus.sh`. Per lane: cheri_riscv64 → SDK `output/sdk/bin/clang++ --target=riscv64-unknown-freebsd`, flag `enable_riscv64_cheri=yes`, token `cheri_riscv64`, sysroot = the env.sh `CHERI_SYSROOT_*` pin (an earlier draft guessed `output/sdk/sysroot-riscv64-purecap`; the pinned value is the path **observed** from the built tree in Step 6, not that guess); aarch64-morello → `output/morello-sdk/bin/clang++ --target=aarch64-unknown-freebsd`, flag `enable_aarch64_morello=yes`, token `aarch64`, sysroot = the env.sh `CHERI_SYSROOT_*` pin likewise. **The sysroot pin is consumed, not merely defined**: the purecap sysroot has no consumer unless a command carries it — verified in-session, a bare `--target=riscv64-unknown-freebsd -march=rv64gcxcheri -mabi=l64pc128d` compile resolves `#include <stdio.h>` against the *host* include root and dies (`bits/libc-header-start.h file not found`). So every CHERI cross-compile passes it explicitly: the corpus unit builds, the cross-built `run-test`, and `lib/common/libcfi.so` use `CXX="<sdk>/bin/clang++ --sysroot=<the lane's CHERI_SYSROOT_* pin>"` (a per-lane wrapper beside the objdump shim is the alternative, if the MAKEFLAGS composition gets unwieldy). The corpus/unit builds (and the cross-built `run-test` + `lib/common/libcfi.so` staged for the guest, riscv64 `build_image.sh` model) carry `ARCH=<token> <enable flag> CXX/ASM/OBJDUMP/disable_independent_assembly=yes`; both MAKEFLAGS strings recorded ONCE (sys_info + the run-side export cannot diverge). **Extend `scripts/common/build_corpus.sh`**: lane case for both bench tokens, toolchain parameterization per lane (CXX/ASM/OBJDUMP + shim dir), and a `--bring-up` tolerant mode. The tolerant mode must cover **all three** abort paths, not just the unit loop — the unit-failure exit (`build_corpus.sh:149-152`), the side-file staging loop's `exit 1` (`:156-164`, "derived side file … was not produced by any unit build") and the finalize step's `sys.exit` on un-staged units/side files (`:184-198`). This matters because the CHERI lanes' predicted failure class *is* the missing-side-file case: `test/acc-read-func-func-opcode.tmp` is a byproduct of the `acc-read-func.gen` unit whose extraction is expected to fail on these lanes, so tolerating only the unit loop still hard-exits the corpus build — the exact dead end the mode exists to prevent. In tolerant mode an unproduced side file is recorded as a non-built manifest entry and its sha256 finalize is skipped; `exit 1` is reserved for strict mode (final acceptance uses strict mode). **Objdump shim boundary** (call it out + record the decision): cheri_riscv64 → host `riscv64-linux-gnu-objdump` (GNU format reads FreeBSD ELF); aarch64-morello → decide between an llvm-objdump→GNU adapter or excluding `acc-read-func` via the Step 10 skip map with a documented bench-side reason. Guest payload staged: `run-test`, derived `configure.json`, `libcfi.so`, the `test/` unit binaries + side files, and `scripts/cheri/guest/runner`.
   - **run.sh** mirrors `scripts/riscv64/run.sh` exactly in structure — lane lock, `timeout "$LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT"`, pre-boot **image hash assert against the env.sh pin**, boot with the boot argv **exactly as Step 7 settled it** (riscv64 passes `-cpu "$QEMU_CPU_CHERI_RISCV64"` — the Sstc workaround — plus **both** `-bios "$QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64"` and `-kernel "$QEMU_KERNEL_CHERI_RISCV64"`; Morello passes `-bios "$QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_MORELLO"` and **no** `-kernel`) under the pinned emulator plus `-snapshot` (or a fresh qcow2 overlay) so the built artifact stays byte-identical, the Step 7 mechanism both ways, `guest-status.txt` handshake (fixed-pattern `grep`, never `eval`/`source` of guest-controlled text), collection loop accepting only regular files (`[ -f "$f" ] && [ ! -L "$f" ]`) **from a fixed filename list, with every collected path anchored** — assert `realpath` stays under the share root and pass `--` before operands, since a guest can otherwise plant a name whose target escapes the share (with `security_model=mapped-xattr` a link target is stored as file contents rather than a host symlink, so the leaf guard suffices for the fixed list, but the invariant is stated rather than assumed; any tree-walk collection uses `find -P -type f`, never `-L`), EXIT-trap collection into `results/<lane>/<stamp>/`, appends emulator-name/version + image lines to the stamp's `versions.txt`, `make -C benchmark clean` + submodule-clean assert on every exit path, 124 publishes nothing, completeness gate against `configure.json`.
   - **Makefile flip** (`Makefile:202-244`): real recipes. `deploy` runs, in order: **sources-if-missing** (`stage_sources.sh`, so a cold clone works — the gitignored `build/` never travels, and without this leg the next step dies and the plan's own "`make deploy` must work on a cold clone" claim is false) → **emulator-if-missing** → **image-if-missing-and-hash-mismatched** (hash-aware, not existence-aware, per the image/pin feedback loop) → **prepare**. **The timeout layering is the point**: only the cheap legs (sources, prepare) are wrapped in `timeout $(LANE_DEPLOY_TIMEOUT)`; the emulator and image script invocations are wrapped in `timeout $(CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT)` instead, because a 7200 s outer cap would fire mid-world-build (exit 124) and make the inner `CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT` dead. `enable_riscv64_cheri=yes` / `enable_aarch64_morello=yes` are forwarded; `run` additionally forwards `LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT=$(LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT)` so the overridable pin actually reaches `run.sh` through its `env.sh` re-source; `clean` unchanged (and still does **not** reclaim `build/cheri` — see Rollback); `dump` keeps an explicit unsupported message. The Makefile's own header comment, which still documents the two lanes as scaffolds, is updated in the same edit (the audit flagged it as left behind by the flip).
   → coder verify (scripted): `python3 ./tmp/verification/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-wiring.py` — the lane scripts' structure (hash assert, `-snapshot`, path anchoring, riscv64 `-bios` + `-kernel` + disk vs morello firmware-from-disk with **no** `-kernel`, lane lock, 124), the Makefile flip (no "not provisioned", sources leg, timeout layering, `LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT` forwarding), and the two shared-builder extensions (`--env-lane`, `--bring-up` covering unit/side-file/finalize, `--sysroot`). Proven red against the pre-change tree (16/19 fail). Runs in the second coder round, after Steps 1–3 are complete.

   → tester verify: a scratch corpus build with one forced unit failure whose derived side file is absent finalizes the manifest and exits 0 in `--bring-up` mode (strict mode still exits non-zero); the 124-no-publish exercise works through the overridable `LANE_RUN_TIMEOUT`
9. **Step 9:** README + docs — planner-owned (see Guidance for Planner); coder only reports the final usage strings.
   → Evidence: the planner's README diff exists and rewrites the intro CHERI bullet (README.md:13-14) and adds the Pins rows (README.md:97-112)
10. **Step 10:** Bring-up acceptance + skip classification (coder + tester). **Prerequisites, both mechanical:** (i) the submodule is checked out at the pinned `c2c7039` — per the user's 2026-10-15 decision this plan stays at the pin (B1's `db84315` bump and the all-lane re-runs are its successor's scope), so HEAD must be moved *to* the pin, not left ahead of it; (ii) `git -C benchmark status --short` is **empty as a pre-run precondition**, untracked paths included — the submodule currently carries four (`.claude/agents`, `.claude/commands`, `.idea`, `.vscode`), and `--short` reports them, so the ignored/untracked set is settled before the run rather than asserted after it. Run each lane once (`deploy` if not yet, then `run`); collect the tester's classification; inject the **per-(lane,record) skip table** into `scripts/common/derive_registry.py`.
    **Keying (the audit found the drafted rule unimplementable):** `--lane` stays the **bench token** (line 34's rule, and the only lane input `derive_registry.py` has — morello's `prepare.sh` passes `aarch64`), so an "env-lane key" could never match. The table is therefore looked up through a **new `--env-lane` argument, defaulting to `--lane`**, which `prepare.sh` passes explicitly for the CHERI lanes (`--env-lane aarch64-morello`); the *emitted skip value* is still the bench token (`cheri_riscv64`; morello emits `aarch64`, safe because the skip lands only in that lane's own derived registry, per-lane out-dir). **No `VALID_LANES` change** — `cheri_riscv64` and `aarch64` are already members, so the drafted instruction was a no-op. The table stays strictly lane-conditional (the riscv64 derived output must stay byte-identical), restricted to build-failure / recognized-bench-extraction-gap / unsupported-mechanism classes, every entry carrying its raw exit code + the tester's classification row as evidence in the plan appendix, skip-reason naming the lane; **forbid skipping anything the classifier filed as a genuine defect** (those become deferrals instead). An assertion at prepare/run time checks the skip token equals the `CSB_TARGET_ARCH` the scheduler was built with. Confirmatory re-run: `make deploy` (regenerating the derived registry) then `make run`; classify again — no unexplained entries. Contingency: if B1 Step 4e fired (no valid morello purecap body), the morello lane's two asmfunc records are permanent skips within this plan's scope with a skip-reason naming B1's deferral, and acceptance of those two records defers with B1's successor.
   → coder verify (auto): each CHERI lane's **derived `configure.json` is grepped for its expected skip entries** before the acceptance run — a keying miss fails here rather than after the multi-hour runs; the re-run's classify output has no unclassified entries; `versions.txt` in each stamp names emulator + guest image; both `results/<lane>/<stamp>/results.json` carry the full registry record set; the riscv64 derived output is byte-identical to its pre-change form
   → tester verify: lanes + classify scripts pass (including the 124-no-publish assertion and the migrated step1.py/step2.py re-runs); the tester's report carries the classification table and the smoke-vs-acceptance boundary

## Guidance for Planner

Two things you execute yourself: the **hosted network/build steps** and the **documentation**.

**1. Hosted execution (Steps 4–6) — your lane, per the user's 2026-10-14 decision.**

- **What you run:** `scripts/cheri/stage_sources.sh` (Step 4, foreground — minutes), then `scripts/cheri/build_emulator.sh` (Step 5) and `scripts/cheri/build_image.sh` (Step 6) as **detached background jobs** with a Monitor armed on each log under `./tmp/logs/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-<step>.log`.
- **Invoke the hosting driver through `bash`** (found 2026-10-15 by attempting attempt 6): `bash tmp/verification/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-run.sh <script> <log>`. The driver is mode `664`, not executable — matching this repo's convention that every script is invoked as `bash <path>`, and the plan forbids `chmod` — so calling it as `./…host-run.sh` fails immediately with `Permission denied` / **exit 126** before the log file is even created. The failure is at the shell, not in the build: if the log is absent, suspect the invocation, not the build.
- **Sequence:** after the coder round that writes Steps 1–3 (scripts must exist and pass their dry-run checks first), and before the step that consumes the artifacts.
- **Boundary:** you author **nothing** outside `./tmp/` — no source, config or doc file. The scripts are the coder's; you execute them and record evidence. Writing build outputs under `build/` is the user-directed hosting role (same pattern as the 2026-10-13 LLVM staging), not plan implementation.
- **On failure:** a failed build is a planner blocker — report the failing step with its log tail; never add a retry loop inside the scripts and never guess at a fix. Files a plan revision instead.
- **Ask before installing host packages.** The known set is **not** one fallback — it is the **five** packages in **Host Prerequisites** (audit-verified missing; `libcap-ng-dev` was found by executing Step 5), all gating, and installing them is the user's action. Surface the whole set at once; never install silently.
- **Host changes are not only packages.** The **glibc-2.43 C23 shim** (2026-10-15) was a user decision taken before any script changed — it restores the pre-C23 declaration where a deliberate upstream `-Werror` check would otherwise reject it, which is never the planner's call to make silently. (The `bsd/bsd.h` multiarch symlink was the other such decision; it is **retired** with the makefs fork and `rm /usr/include/bsd` reverses it.) Escalate that class rather than writing it through.

**2. Documentation.**

- **Files to modify:**
  - `./README.md`
- **When:** after the coder finishes (before final revision), plus Step 9's content.
- **What to sync (the audit found three stale spots beyond the two originally listed):**
  - (a) the **intro** CHERI bullet (README.md:13-14) — both lanes are live, with the stage-sources → build-emulator → build-image → deploy/run chain; drop "full bring-up is deferred".
  - (b) the **Known caveats** CHERI bullet (README.md:140-144) — it still claims `make ARCH=cheri_riscv64|aarch64-morello deploy|run` "prints 'not provisioned' and exits non-zero" and names the open deferral. Both claims die with this plan (Step 8 asserts `grep -c 'not provisioned' Makefile` = 0; the deferral is retired). Rewrite it, folding in the workarounds note.
  - (c) the **Pins table** (README.md:97-112) — append rows for `CHERI_QEMU_COMMIT`, `CHERIBSD_SRC_COMMIT` (**and `BBL_SRC_COMMIT`**, added 2026-10-16 — the riscv64 BBL firmware's riscv-pk pin; there is **no** `MAKEFS_SRC_COMMIT` row, that pin was removed 2026-10-15), the emulator binary pins, the per-lane disk-image pins, **the two firmware pins** (`QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_MORELLO` and `QEMU_BIOS_CHERI_RISCV64` — both bare datadir filenames), the per-lane purecap sysroot pins (`CHERI_SYSROOT_*`, analogous to the existing sysroot row at :105), the CHERI boot-parameter pins (extend the "QEMU CPU models" row at :107 with the two lanes' machine/CPU/memory pins), and `CHERI_BUILD_TIMEOUT`/`CHERI_DEPLOY_TIMEOUT` in the timeouts row (:112); qualify the existing QEMU 10.2.1 row as the non-CHERI lanes' emulator.
  - (d) the **Output layout** block (README.md:82-89) — the stamp-contents annotation says boot.log/guest-status.txt apply "on riscv64" only and defines `versions.txt` as env.sh pins + dpkg versions + uname; the CHERI lane stamps also carry `guest-status.txt` and append emulator-name/version and the guest disk-image line.
  - (e) the **Prerequisites** section (README.md:21-30) — add the five host packages from Host Prerequisites with their one-line reasons.
  - (f) note the bring-up workarounds (`disable_independent_assembly`, per-lane skip table) and that the no-workaround acceptance follows the successor sweep.
  - (g) record the two lanes' **differing boot lines** (riscv64 `-kernel` + disk; morello UEFI + `loader(8)` from disk) so a future reader does not "simplify" riscv64 to a disk-only boot — that path has no transition that can succeed.
- **Deferral retirement:** this plan resolves `./tmp/reports/defer-issue-cheri-lanes-bringup-deferred.json` — `/update-and-commit` Step 9.6 retires it when the Issue Log row reaches Resolved. The reviewer hunch row (`h-cheri-lane-flip-not-done`) is in-plan and resolves with Step 8.
- **Successor coordination (write it into the plan's History at finalization):** the no-workaround acceptance (bench at the B1 commit, `disable_independent_assembly` removed, full corpus) belongs to the B1 follow-up sweep; if the user prefers, it can instead be a short C1 continuation plan. State which was chosen.

| 2026-10-16 | **Round 26 closed the firmware gap, hit the Sstc panic — and the user chose to stop advertising Sstc, A/B-verified by the planner** | **Round 26 (12:08–12:56, exit 0)** is the first round since 22 to write **both** its session report (`build_result: fail`) and an issue. riscv64 **got past firmware load** — `bbl loader` → kernel → FDT → full device probe → `Device configuration finished.` — so round 24's missing-firmware gap is closed — then **panicked at clock init**: `Illegal instruction 0x14d51073 at 0xffffffc00070769a`, disassembled to `csrw stimecmp, a0` in `riscv_timer_et_start` (the `DELAY+0x43c` attribution is mis-symbolization, checked against the pinned kernel). **Morello booted to `login:` on the current reassembled pins** (380 s). The chain, all point-of-effect: the DT advertises Sstc (QEMU's `sstc` CPU property defaults on) → the kernel sets `has_sstc` (`identcpu.c:466`) → `csr_write(stimecmp)` (`timer.c:92-93`) → QEMU gates that CSR on `mcounteren.TM` **and** `menvcfg.STCE` (`csr.c:886-889`, per spec) → **BBL writes `mcounteren` but never `menvcfg`** (`minit.c:71`) → illegal instruction. The candidate tests were not run and `versions-spike.txt` deliberately not created; hashes asserted unchanged; the guest runner was written. **User decision 2026-10-16: stop advertising Sstc** — `QEMU_CPU_CHERI_RISCV64="rv64,sstc=off"` — over patching the pinned riscv-pk (provenance deviation + the SDK-changed rebuild that would move both image pins again) or swapping to CHERI OpenSBI (bigger provenance change). **The planner verified it before briefing:** a two-arm diagnostic on the same pinned artifacts (`tmp/verification/2026-10-16-cheri-lanes-bringup-diag-sstc.sh`) — **control → the exact round-26 panic; `rv64,sstc=off` → `login:`; disk sha256 byte-identical afterwards**. The spelling is the lane's own model (`virt`'s `default_cpu_type` *is* the concrete `rv64`), so CHERI is untouched — proven by a purecap kernel booting under it — and the kernel's `sbi_set_timer` fallback is implemented by this BBL (`mtrap.c:92,171`). The makefs blocker that killed attempt 5 is **gone**, and the mandatory **security pre-seed was verified at the point of effect**: the staged `extra-files/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` (0600) holds exactly one key, byte-identical to `insecure_test_ssh_key.pub`, and because it exists `disk_image.py:521`'s `is_file()` guard means the personal-`~/.ssh/*.pub` block **never ran**. The value arrives by inheritance: `env.sh:56` pins it for the bench's own builds, **`env.sh:85` exports it**, and `build_image.sh:60` sources `env.sh`. **riscv64 escaped only because its world was built by the C23 diagnostic, which invoked cheribuild directly and never sourced `env.sh`** — attempt 5 reused that world in 43 s and never re-ran `newvers.sh`; Morello is the first *fresh* world build under this environment. **Proven at the point of effect**: the exact failing command with the pin set → `Invalid SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH`/exit 1; with it empty → exit 0 and `vers.c` written. **User decision 2026-10-15: "scope it off for the CHERI legs"** — an **empty** `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=` on the two CheriBSD `run_cheribuild` invocations, leaving the shared `env.sh` pin and export untouched (verified no in-repo consumer needs the export: `riscv64/build_image.sh` parses `env.sh` as a file, `build_busybox.sh:29` self-exports, nothing in the Makefile or bench reads it). **A trap found while verifying the fix, before briefing it:** a **second `--cheribsd/extra-env` flag OVERWRITES the first** — `--pretend` shows the two-flag spelling resolving to `['SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=']` with **`CPATH` silently gone**, which would have re-broken stage 1.1; both entries must ride in one space-separated value, verified resolving to `['CPATH=<shim>', 'SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH=']` and appearing together on 7 `make.py` command lines. | 2026-10-15 | Spec revision — the **CWARNFLAGS waiver was ineffective; replaced by a verified CPATH C23 shim** (attempt 4) | The waiver landed exactly as briefed (coder round 11, verifier 67/67) and **did not work**: attempt 4 died at the same place with a **byte-identical compile command** still carrying `-Werror=incompatible-pointer-types-discards-qualifiers`. Diagnosis: the variable reached the **top-level** make — it is on the printed `make.py buildworld` line — but **FreeBSD's build does not propagate arbitrary command-line variables into the `tools/build` sub-make**, which recomputes `CWARNFLAGS.clang` from scratch (every `bsd.sys.mk` entry present, every `Makefile.boot` entry present, ours absent — nothing even appended). Because `Makefile.boot:41` uses `+=` **last**, no external value — command line, environment, or `src-env.conf` — can ever win: **the entire "set the make variable" family is dead, not just that spelling.** **Root cause of the root cause, recorded deliberately:** the planner verified the *injection point* (bmake override semantics in one make; `--pretend` showing the option on the top-level make) and never the *effect* (the compile command). That hop is where the fix lived. **Revision 1 is superseded, not patched.** The replacement is a **CPATH header shim** (`scripts/cheri/glibc-c23-shim/{string.h,wchar.h,stdlib.h}` + `CHERI_CROSS_CPATH`, injected via `--cheribsd/extra-env="CPATH=…"`): CPATH is read by the **compiler**, so it needs no make cooperation, and it is **self-scoping** (CPATH is searched after every `-I`, and the cross-build stage takes `<string.h>` from its own sysroot). Each shim `#include_next`s glibc's header and `#undef`s the C23 macro layer, restoring the C17 declaration that sits directly above each macro; the 11 symbols across 3 headers are the **complete** const-generic set, enumerated from glibc's own headers. **Proven end-to-end at the point of effect before briefing** — a hosted diagnostic carried `>>> stage 1.1` through to `>>> stage 4.2` with no diagnostics from `getopt*.c` (log `./tmp/logs/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-diag-cpath.log`). `CCC_OVERRIDE_OPTIONS` was tested and **rejected** (works, but 2 lines per clang invocation — tens of thousands across a world build). The dead pin is **removed**, not left in place, and its removal is asserted. Changes: Immediate Actions carries the four-edit brief; the verifier is rewritten to **72 checks, red at 63/72**; Host Prerequisites' workaround subsection corrected; Step 3's bullet and Step 6's Evidence clause corrected. Evidence: `./tmp/logs/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-image-attempt4-findings.txt`. | — |
| 2026-10-15 | Spec revision (/update-plan) — the **glibc-2.43 C23 waiver** (Step 6 attempt 3 blocker; user decision) | **Attempt 3 cleared `makefs-linux` and entered the real world build** (`>>> World build started … 12:12:39` → `>>> stage 1.1`), confirming round 9's world-first ordering fix — then died ~7 min in with `*** [getopt.o] Error code 1` / `*** [legacy] Error code 2` / `Fatal error (in target cheribsd-riscv64-purecap)`. **Root cause (verified, not inferred):** this host's **glibc 2.43** declares `strchr` as a C23 `_Generic` macro returning `const char *` for a const argument (`/usr/include/string.h:261-265`, guard `__GLIBC_USE (ISOC23)`, reproduced with a direct compile), while FreeBSD's bootstrap makefile promotes that const-discard to an error unconditionally (`tools/build/mk/Makefile.boot:41`). The Linux cross-build shim is unaware of the C23 macros. **Environmental — neither ours nor the pin's:** CheriBSD `main` (pushed 2026-10-14) *still carries the flag*, so a pin bump buys nothing, and the identical breakage hit lxcfs (GH #707) and Gentoo's glibc-2.43 tracker (#970449). **User decision 2026-10-15: "tolerate the one warning"** — keep the pinned sources, neutralize the single flag via `--cheribsd/build-options`. Verified before briefing (never guessed): bmake command-line assignment suppresses the makefile's later `+=` (scratch-Makefile test → `RESULT[-Wno-error=discards]`), and `--pretend` shows `CWARNFLAGS.clang=…` landing on `buildworld`/`buildkernel`/`installworld`/`installsysroot`/`installkernel` in the real three-target invocation (exit 0). **Trap found and documented:** cheribuild splits list options on whitespace, so a two-flag value degenerates into a bare make argument — the pin must be one space-free token. **Accepted cost, stated when the user chose:** `CWARNFLAGS.clang` is shared with `bsd.sys.mk`'s ~14 entries, so overriding it drops FreeBSD's clang warning policy build-wide — log noise, no correctness change (no global bare `-Werror` is active). **Escalated rather than written through**, per the stop rule: this is the third "bleeding-edge host toolchain vs. pinned source" failure (libcap-ng, GCC 15/makefs, now glibc 2.43) and the fix weakens a deliberate upstream check — the user's call, and taken. Changes: Immediate Actions carries the two-edit coder brief; the planner-owned verifier gains four checks (**67, red at 63/67**); Host Prerequisites gains the waiver subsection; Risks gains the host-toolchain-drift row. Evidence: `./tmp/logs/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-host-image-attempt3-findings.txt`. | — |
| 2026-10-15 | Spec revision — **Step 1's staging design was wrong; rewritten to git-based staging (user decision A)** | Executing hosted Step 5 (attempt 4, 09:25) proved the plan's central staging premise false. **cheribuild cannot accept a non-git source tree:** `Project.update()` calls `ensure_cloned()` *before* its `if current_project.skip_update: return` (`project.py:1354-1362`) and `GitRepository.ensure_cloned` clones whenever `(src_dir/".git")` is absent (`repository.py:371-390`) — so `--skip-update` never prevents a clone, and `--skip-clone` only turns it into a fatal `Sources for … missing!`. The failure was literal: `cd / && git clone --depth 1 --no-single-branch --branch main https://github.com/CHERI-Alliance/qemu.git …/sources/qemu` → `fatal: destination path … already exists and is not an empty directory`. **The plan's own Summary was the source of the error**: it claimed `build_toolchains.sh` established a "codeload tarball → pre-stage → `--skip-update`" pattern, but that script tarball-fetches *cheribuild itself* and only *asserts* the LLVM trees exist; those trees — the only staging this repo has proven — are git clones (`llvm-project/.git` → `origin = github.com/CTSRD-CHERI/llvm-project.git`). **Verified the fix before proposing it**: scratch repo `build/cheri/scratch/gitprobe-1` confirmed `git init` + `fetch --depth 1 origin <sha>` + `checkout --detach FETCH_HEAD` yields HEAD == the pin with a real `.git`. **User decision 2026-10-15: design (A), git-based staging.** Changes: the Summary's staging-pattern bullet and pin-table note rewritten; Step 1's mechanics, its `env.sh` clause (the three `*_URL` pins become git remotes; commits unchanged) and its coder-verify clause (the `git clone = 0` grep becomes `codeload = 0`) rewritten; the TOFU record's tarball-sha256 becomes the staged tree hash; Immediate Actions carries the rewrite brief; the planner-owned verifier is rewritten to match (**58 checks, red at 53/58**, five git-staging failures). Four earlier defects from this same hosting run were fixed en route and are recorded in Immediate Actions: the `build_emulator.sh:64` empty-`IFS` splitting bug (coder round 6, fixed and verified 57/57), the missing `libcap-ng-dev` (user-installed), the conda `python3` shadowing (hosting driver prepends `/usr/bin`), and the `bsd.h` multiarch path (user symlink). **Recurring finding: every one of these was invisible to the structural verifier — none of these scripts had ever been executed.** | — |

**+1** for round 29's own outcome, which produced the **harvest rule** (detaching protects the boot but does not license ending before the verdicts are appended; poll repeatedly, ~5 min then every ~2 min) and the **driver-prompt defect** (shell-independent prompt detection, and no wait without a timeout) — both of which changed the round-30 brief; **+1** for the third acceptance run, which showed the **prompt gate itself is insufficient** (a completion prompt can precede the token echo, and a duplicate's prompt can precede the queued command) — the round-37 brief removes the re-send entirely and the Step 10 acceptance brief moves to round 38. ## Final Results

**Status: COMPLETED — 2026-10-17.** The two CHERI lanes are real: `cheri_riscv64` (`qemu-system-riscv64xcheri`) and `aarch64-morello` (`qemu-system-morello`) boot pinned purecap CheriBSD 15.0-CURRENT guests from the pinned disk images, receive the prebuilt cpu-sec-bench corpus through virtio-9p (`bake: NOTHING`), run all 246 derived records in-guest, and publish complete stamps under `results/<lane>/<UTC-stamp>/`. The predecessor deferral [cheri-lanes-bringup-deferred](./tmp/reports/defer-issue-cheri-lanes-bringup-deferred.json) is closed by this plan.

### Acceptance evidence

- **Final stamps (run 6, the committed state):** `results/cheri_riscv64/20261017T132840Z/` and `results/aarch64-morello/20261017T135359Z/` — 246 records each, `guest-status.txt` = `run-test-exit: 0`, `1025` skip counts exactly **4 / 3** (the two asmfunc deferrals plus each lane's recorded corpus build failures), manifests **141 / 142 units with zero non-built**, one driver sentinel with **zero re-sends and zero sync fallbacks** per lane, and each console's `# driver argv:` line carrying the emulator path exactly once.
- **Final tester round: SUCCESS 8/8** — `./tmp/reports/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-tester-2026-10-17-run6.json`; classification **a=0, b=2, c=25, d=0**, no skip candidates, no unexplained entries; artifact `./tmp/verification/2026-10-14-cheri-lanes-bringup-classification-run6.json` (sha `10e3885e…`), with the run-4 and run-5 tables preserved as baselines (sha `59f370d9…`, `9ee647fe…`).
- **Prior acceptance, tester-verified:** run 4 (`20261017T073711Z` / `20261017T080425Z`, SUCCESS 10/10) and run 5 (`20261017T085733Z` / `20261017T092154Z`, SUCCESS 8/8).
- **Structural verifiers:** `...-scripts.py` **114/114**, `...-wiring.py` **32/32**; the shared riscv64 corpus regression (`...-test-env-all-archs-step2.py`) green; the derive regression byte-identical (`configure.json` `ec9b9206…`, `manifest.json` `d5c3f2a5…`).
- **Integrity:** `benchmark` clean at `c2c7039` (no `BENCH_COMMIT` change); both disk images byte-identical to their `env.sh` pins (`afdcb05d…`, `00437fdd…`); every boot `-snapshot` with a timeout; guest networking `-net none` enforced fail-closed; no host packages installed by the planner; no `chmod` changes; only the test SSH public key is baked.

### Residual risks

- The bring-up lanes build the corpus with `disable_independent_assembly=yes`; the asmfunc deferral is explicit and skippable, not silent.
- `prepare.sh` uses the tolerant `--bring-up` mode; strict mode is tested but not wired into `make deploy` (review suggestion).
- Guest network containment depends on the `env.sh` pins, now enforced fail-closed at lane start.
